<tr><td>biodynamics</td><td>bīōdȳnamics</td><td>bio-dyn-am-ic-s</td><td>bīō-dȳn-am-ic-s</td><td>n. the study of the effects of dynamic processes on living organisms</td></tr>
<tr><td>electrodynamic</td><td>ėlectrōdȳnamic</td><td>electro-dyn-am-ic</td><td>ėlectrō-dȳn-am-ic</td><td>a. of or relating to electrodynamics</td></tr>
<tr><td>electrodynamics</td><td>ėlectrōdȳnamics</td><td>electro-dyn-am-ic-s</td><td>ėlectrō-dȳn-am-ic-s</td><td>n. the physics of electric currents and their associated magnetic fields</td></tr>
<tr><td>psychodynamic</td><td>𝕡sȳc𝕙ōdȳnamic</td><td>psycho-dyn-am-ic</td><td>𝕡sȳc𝕙ō-dȳn-am-ic</td><td>a. relating to the interaction of various psychological forces</td></tr>
<tr><td>psychodynamics</td><td>𝕡sȳc𝕙ōdȳnamics</td><td>psycho-dyn-am-ics</td><td>𝕡sȳc𝕙ō-dȳn-am-ics</td><td>n. the interrelation of conscious and unconscious processes determining personality</td></tr>
<tr><td>aerodynamically</td><td>āerōdȳnamicållẏ</td><td>aer-o-dyn-am-ic-al-ly</td><td>āer-ō-dȳn-am-ic-ål-lẏ</td><td>adv. in a manner related to aerodynamics</td></tr>
<tr><td>chromodynamics</td><td>c𝕙rōmo̊dȳnamics</td><td>chrom-o-dyn-am-ics</td><td>c𝕙rōm-o̊-dȳn-am-ics</td><td>n. the branch of quantum physics that studies the interactions of quarks</td></tr>
<tr><td>dyno</td><td>dȳnō</td><td>dyno</td><td>dȳnō</td><td>n. short for dynamometer; a device for measuring force or power</td></tr>
